158	```python
159	from artagents.orchestrate import code, nested, plan
160	from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file
161	
162	hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
163	    code("transcribe", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe", "..."], produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")}),
164	    code("cut", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut", "..."], produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")}),
165	    code("render", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render", "..."], produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")}),
166	    nested("thumbnail", plan="builtin.thumbnail_maker", produces={"thumbnail": file_nonempty("thumbnail.png")}),
167	])
168	```
169	
170	Sub-orchestrator delegation appears only as `nested`. Child `prepare_project_run()` calls inherit `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID`, skip child `run.json`, and mirror produces under parent `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/`; standalone behavior stays unchanged.
171	
172	## 14. Documentation Deliverables
173	
174	| Document | Audience | Size target | Content |
175	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
176	| `AUTHORING.md` | Human and LLM authors | About one page | DSL, verify helpers, compile/check/test, and code-vs-nested boundary. |
177	| `AGENT.md` template | In-flight agent | Short run contract | Dropped into `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` with task-mode rules and preamble. |
178	| `README.md` updates | Operators | Quickstart | `start`, `next`, `ack`, `status`, and recovery examples. |
179	| `AGENTS.md` update | Agents | Cross-link | Points to `AUTHORING.md` and repeats qualified `<pack>.<orch>` ids. |
180	
181	## 15. Risk Register
182	
183	| Risk | Trigger | Mitigation |
184	| --- | --- | --- |
185	| Single-host assumption | No daemon means two operators can race. | Validate the chain before each command and print recovery commands. |
186	| Semantic verifier discipline | Authors regress to sentinel-existence checks. | `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs. |
187	| Context-decay re-injection | Long sessions forget prohibitions. | Stop-hook nudge calls `next`; every response repeats the preamble. |
188	| Honor-model boundary | A malicious local agent edits files. | State plainly that hash chains are logs and tripwires, not sandboxing. |
189	| V1 infrastructure creep | A daemon, web server, HMAC, `.index.db`, or shared CAS is proposed as a shortcut. | Reject it for V1 and keep the design single-host, file-based, plain-hash, file-walk, and per-project. |
190	
191	## 16. Cut Points
192	
193	Phase 3 ships a useful frozen-plan runner: gate, three step kinds, produces checks, and repeat/fan-out. Phase 5 ships the author-friendly CLI surface. Phases 6-9 are polish and scale: nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden author tests.
194	
195	## 17. Open-Call Confirmations
196	
197	- A1 Confirmed: nested stays data visible to the gate, and `code` argv to `artagents orchestrators run` is forbidden.
198	- A2 Confirmed: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed representation, and `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored.
199	- A3 Confirmed: V1 drops `.index.db` and ships with direct file-walk verbs.
200	- A4 Confirmed: kernel modules land in new `artagents/core/task/` files above the legacy runners.
201	- A5 Confirmed: lifecycle, operator, and author verbs extend top-level `artagents/pipeline.py:15`; existing `artagents orchestrators` verbs stay unchanged.
202	- A6 Confirmed: `code` is deterministic argv subprocess execution, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
203	- A7 Confirmed: the gate runs before both `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()`, with zero project-run side effects on rejection.
204	- A8 Confirmed: root `.gitignore` excludes pack-local `<pack>/build/` outputs.
205	- A9 Confirmed: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the inheritance env surface across orchestrator, executor, hype, project-run, and thread-wrapper code paths.
206	- A10 Confirmed: task mode skips child `run.json` records but preserves child output dirs and hype artifact mirroring under parent step produces.
207	- A11 Confirmed: `artagents/orchestrate/` and `artagents/verify/` are new top-level packages accepted by structure checks.
208	
209	## Settled Decisions
210	
211	SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
212	
213	SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
214	
215	SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
216	
217	SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
218	
219	SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
```

```
1	"""Module loader and JSON compiler for the orchestrate DSL (Phase 4).
2	
3	`resolve_orchestrator(qid)` imports `<packs_root>/<pack>/<name>.py` via
4	`importlib.util.spec_from_file_location` (without polluting sys.modules across
5	test fixtures) and returns the module-level ``_PlanBuilder``.
6	
7	`compile_to_path(qid)` resolves, calls ``_PlanBuilder.to_dict()`` (which
8	round-trips through ``artagents.core.task.plan.load_plan``), and writes the
9	manifest to ``<pack-root>/build/<name>.json`` as deterministic JSON.
10	
11	Inline expansion of nested string-form refs (``nested(plan="<pack>.<name>")``)
12	runs during ``to_dict()``: ``compile_to_path`` passes itself in as the
13	``_resolver`` and threads a ``_visiting`` set keyed on qualified id so a
14	self- or mutually-recursive reference raises ``OrchestrateDefinitionError``
15	instead of recursing forever (FLAG-005).
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import importlib.util
21	import json
22	import sys
23	import uuid
24	from pathlib import Path
25	from typing import Optional
26	
27	from artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT
28	
29	from .dsl import OrchestrateDefinitionError, _PlanBuilder
30	
31	DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT = REPO_ROOT / "artagents" / "packs"
32	
33	
34	def _qualified_split(qualified_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
35	    if not isinstance(qualified_id, str) or not qualified_id:
36	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
37	            "qualified id must be a non-empty string of the form '<pack>.<name>'"
38	        )
39	    if "." not in qualified_id:
40	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
41	            f"qualified id {qualified_id!r} must be '<pack>.<name>'"
42	        )
43	    pack, _, name = qualified_id.partition(".")
44	    if not pack or not name or "." in name:
45	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
46	            f"qualified id {qualified_id!r} must be exactly '<pack>.<name>' "
47	            "(no extra dots)"
48	        )
49	    return pack, name
50	
51	
52	def _load_module_isolated(module_path: Path, qualified_id: str):
53	    unique = f"_artagents_orchestrate_{qualified_id.replace('.', '_')}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
54	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(unique, module_path)
55	    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
56	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
57	            f"could not load orchestrator module at {module_path}"
58	        )
59	    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
60	    sys.modules[unique] = module
61	    try:
62	        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
63	    except Exception as exc:
64	        sys.modules.pop(unique, None)
65	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
66	            f"failed to import orchestrator module {module_path}: {exc}"
67	        ) from exc
68	    finally:
69	        sys.modules.pop(unique, None)
70	    return module
71	
72	
73	def resolve_orchestrator(
74	    qualified_id: str,
75	    *,
76	    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,
77	    _visiting: Optional[set] = None,
78	) -> _PlanBuilder:
79	    pack, name = _qualified_split(qualified_id)
80	    root = Path(packs_root) if packs_root is not None else DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT
81	    pack_root = root / pack
82	    if not pack_root.is_dir():
83	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
84	            f"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: pack directory not found at {pack_root}"
85	        )
86	    module_path = pack_root / f"{name}.py"
87	    if not module_path.is_file():
88	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
89	            f"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module file not found at {module_path}"
90	        )
91	    module = _load_module_isolated(module_path, qualified_id)
92	
93	    builders: list[_PlanBuilder] = [
94	        value
95	        for value in vars(module).values()
96	        if isinstance(value, _PlanBuilder)
97	    ]
98	    if not builders:
99	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
100	            f"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module {module_path} defines no "
101	            "_PlanBuilder (use plan(...) or @orchestrator(...))"
102	        )
103	    matching = [b for b in builders if b.plan_id == qualified_id]
104	    if len(matching) == 1:
105	        return matching[0]
106	    if len(matching) > 1:
107	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
108	            f"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module {module_path} defines "
109	            f"multiple plans with plan_id {qualified_id!r}"
110	        )
111	    if len(builders) == 1:
112	        return builders[0]
113	    ids = sorted({b.plan_id for b in builders})
114	    raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
115	        f"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module {module_path} defines multiple "
116	        f"plans {ids}; declare exactly one with plan_id={qualified_id!r}"
117	    )
118	
119	
120	def _resolver_for(packs_root: Optional[Path]):
121	    def _resolve(qualified_id: str, *, _visiting: Optional[set] = None) -> _PlanBuilder:
122	        return resolve_orchestrator(
123	            qualified_id, packs_root=packs_root, _visiting=_visiting
124	        )
125	
126	    return _resolve
127	
128	
129	def compile_to_path(
130	    qualified_id: str,
131	    *,
132	    dest: Optional[Path] = None,
133	    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,
134	) -> Path:
135	    pack, name = _qualified_split(qualified_id)
136	    builder = resolve_orchestrator(qualified_id, packs_root=packs_root)
137	    payload = builder.to_dict(_resolver=_resolver_for(packs_root))
138	    root = Path(packs_root) if packs_root is not None else DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT
139	    if dest is not None:
140	        out_path = Path(dest)
141	    else:
142	        out_path = root / pack / "build" / f"{name}.json"
143	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
144	    out_path.write_text(
145	        json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
146	        encoding="utf-8",
147	    )
148	    return out_path
149
```

311	                f"json_file(), audio_duration_min(...), image_dimensions(...))"
312	            )
313	        out[name] = (path, check)
314	    return out
315	
316	
317	def _coerce_produces_value(value: Any, step_id: str, name: str) -> Tuple[Check, str]:
318	    """Accept a bare ``Check`` (path defaults to the produces name) or a
319	    ``(check, path)`` tuple. Anything else raises.
320	    """
321	    if isinstance(value, Check):
322	        return value, name
323	    if isinstance(value, tuple) and len(value) == 2:
324	        a, b = value
325	        if isinstance(a, Check) and isinstance(b, str) and b:
326	            return a, b
327	        if isinstance(b, Check) and isinstance(a, str) and a:
328	            return b, a
329	    raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
330	        f"step {step_id!r} produces[{name!r}] must be a Check or (check, path) "
331	        f"tuple, got {type(value).__name__}"
332	    )
333	
334	
335	def _normalize_argv(argv: Any, step_id: str) -> Tuple[str, ...]:
336	    if not isinstance(argv, list) or not argv:
337	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
338	            f"code step {step_id!r} argv must be a non-empty list"
339	        )
340	    out: list = []
341	    for index, element in enumerate(argv):
342	        if isinstance(element, _ProducesHandle):
343	            out.append(str(element))
344	        elif isinstance(element, str):
345	            out.append(element)
346	        else:
347	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
348	                f"code step {step_id!r} argv[{index}] must be str or "
349	                f"_ProducesHandle, got {type(element).__name__}"
350	            )
351	    return tuple(out)
352

354	def _reject_orchestrators_run_argv(argv: Tuple[str, ...], step_id: str) -> None:
355	    tokens = _strip_artagents_prefix(list(argv))
356	    if len(tokens) >= 2 and tokens[0] == "orchestrators" and tokens[1] == "run":
357	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
358	            f"code step {step_id!r} argv targets 'artagents orchestrators run'; "
359	            "use a nested(plan=...) step instead"
360	        )

363	def _normalize_repeat(repeat: Any, step_id: str) -> Optional[_RepeatSpec]:
364	    if repeat is None:
365	        return None
366	    if not isinstance(repeat, (_RepeatUntilSpec, _RepeatForEachSpec)):
367	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
368	            f"step {step_id!r} repeat must come from repeat_until() or "
369	            f"repeat_for_each(), got {type(repeat).__name__}"
370	        )
371	    return repeat

374	def _normalize_ack(ack: Any, step_id: str) -> dict:
375	    if isinstance(ack, str):

377	    elif isinstance(ack, dict) and "kind" in ack:
378	        kind = ack["kind"]
379	    else:
380	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
381	            f"attested step {step_id!r} ack must be 'agent', 'actor', or "
382	            f"a dict with 'kind'; got {type(ack).__name__}"
383	        )
384	    if kind not in _VALID_ACK_KINDS:
385	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
386	            f"attested step {step_id!r} ack.kind must be one of "
387	            f"{sorted(_VALID_ACK_KINDS)}, got {kind!r}"
388	        )
389	    return {"kind": kind}

403	    _reject_orchestrators_run_argv(normalized_argv, step_id)

405	    produces_dict = _normalize_produces(produces, step_id, kind="code")
406	    repeat_spec = _normalize_repeat(repeat, step_id)
407	    return _StepHandle(

Auto-approval correctness is only checked by an isolated unit test and never by the canonical golden run — that's a real regression in test coverage given that auto-approval is the whole point of Phase 9.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        }\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"correctness\",\n      \"question\": \"Are the proposed changes technically correct?\",\n      \"guidance\": \"Look for logic errors, invalid assumptions, broken invariants, schema mismatches, or behavior that would fail at runtime. When the fix adds a conditional branch, check whether it handles all relevant cases — not just the one reported in the issue.\",\n      \"findings\": [\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"The plan's Step 1 hype.py uses `code(\\\"smoke\\\", argv=[\\\"python3\\\", \\\"-c\\\", \\\"import json, sys; json.dump({'ok': True}, sys.stdout)\\\"], produces={\\\"out\\\": file_nonempty()})`. `_run_inline_checks` (gate.py:1129-1131) computes `artifact_path = step_dir / entry.path` where `entry.path` defaults to the produces name (`out`), so the gate looks for a file `step_dir/out`. The argv only writes to stdout — no file is ever created at `step_dir/out` — so the inline `file_nonempty` check fails, the gate emits `produces_check_failed` + `cursor_rewind`, and the test loop never advances. The smoke fixture cannot pass with this argv; either drop produces or have the argv write a real file.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Step 4's auto-approval description says it should skip both `validate_attested_identity` AND `match_attested_command`. `match_attested_command` is the *command-prefix matcher* — not identity validation — and it is what the gate uses to know which step the incoming command targets. Skipping it entirely with a synthetic dispatch is fine if the gate has already located the cursor via `peek_current_step`, but the description conflates the two concerns and could lead the implementer to break command-cursor coupling. Worth a clarifying note before implementation.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"`gate_command` calls `apply_task_run_env` (gate.py:817, 832, 848) which mutates the live process `os.environ` (TASK_RUN_ID, TASK_PROJECT, TASK_STEP_ID, TASK_ITEM_ID, TASK_ITERATION). Running `author test` inside pytest leaks those values across tests, breaking later tests that depend on `is_in_task_run()` returning False or that interact with the actor env. The plan does not specify that `_cmd_test` must restore the prior environ on exit, and existing tests do not currently set these vars.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"The plan strips `hash` from both the captured events and the golden, then writes the normalized form to disk on `--regenerate`. But `gate_command` calls `verify_chain(events_path)` (gate.py:532-534) on every dispatch — so the *on-disk* events.jsonl during a real test run must still contain valid hash-chain entries. The plan only mentions stripping hashes on the diff side, which is correct; just confirm the `--regenerate` writer normalizes (strips hashes) on output rather than touching the live in-flight events.jsonl. The plan's wording on this is ambiguous.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Step 4 says the synthetic attested event should be recorded with `attestor_kind=\\\"agent\\\"` regardless of the step's actual `ack.kind`. If the orchestrator's attested step uses `ack=actor`, an `agent`-kind synthetic ack does not match the design's actor/agent semantics. This may not break the gate (since `derive_cursor` advances on any `step_attested` event), but it does mean goldens cannot detect ack-kind regressions. The plan should pick `attestor_kind` based on `step.ack.kind` for accurate goldens.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"The plan does not address `repeat.until.condition == 'user_approves'` semantics under auto-approval. In normal flow, an attested step inside `repeat.until=user_approves` advances on the first attestation. Auto-approval would terminate the loop at iteration 1 — never exercising iteration_failed/iteration_started. That's probably the intended behavior, but it should be stated; otherwise authors will be surprised when goldens never include later iterations.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        }\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"scope\",\n      \"question\": \"Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?\",\n      \"guidance\": \"Look at how the changed function is used across the codebase. **Critical issues flagged:**
- **FLAG-P9-CRIT-001**: The `code("smoke", argv=[python3 -c "...json.dump to stdout"], produces={"out": file_nonempty()})` will fail every run — the argv writes to stdout but the inline check looks for a file at `step_dir/out`, which is never created. The smoke fixture cannot pass as designed.
- **FLAG-P9-CRIT-002**: Fixture/golden directories in plan Step 2/3 are placed under `packs/builtin/hype/...` but the existing `_cmd_test` and `_cmd_new` resolve under `<pack_root>/fixtures` (i.e. `packs/builtin/`). The CLI cannot find them.
- **FLAG-P9-CRIT-003**: An env-var (`ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`) as the only gate for auto-approval is a production-side hole — `child_subprocess_env` doesn't scrub it, so a stray export turns every attested step in production into a free pass.

**Major:**
- The existing scaffold tests (`test_author_test_scaffold.py`) directly assert the Phase-9 stub messages and rc==2; the rewrite WILL break them, contradicting the plan's "all existing tests pass" promise.
- `apply_task_run_env` mutates `os.environ` in-process, leaking task-run env vars across pytest tests.

**Minor:** ack-kind hardcoded to "agent" loses actor/agent distinction in goldens; CLI shape doesn't match the brief's literal `--pack/--orchestrator` form; `--regenerate` not documented in pipeline help; `.gitignore` audit unmentioned; `repeat.until=user_approves` semantics under auto-approval unstated.

Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revised)

## Overview

Phase 9 transforms the existing Phase 5 scaffold `_cmd_test` (which diffs pre-existing golden/fixture events files) into a full runtime replay: run an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture scratch dir with auto-approval ON, capture the live `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against the golden. The `--regenerate` flag writes the current (normalized) events as the new golden.

**Constraints applied from critique:**
- **Place the new DSL orchestrator at `artagents/packs/builtin/author_test_smoke.py`** with plan_id `builtin.author_test_smoke`. This avoids a same-stem collision with the legacy `builtin/hype/` package (FLAG-003 guard), the double-registration of `builtin.hype` (FLAG-P9-005/scope-2), and the path-scheme divergence (FLAG-P9-CRIT-002/issue_hints-1).
- **Include at least one attested step** in the smoke fixture so auto-approval is exercised by the canonical golden run, not just an isolated unit test (issue_hints-5).
- **The code step MUST produce a file** at the step-dir path expected by produces checks (FLAG-P9-CRIT-001/correctness-1).
- **Scrub `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` from child subprocess env** so it cannot leak into production processes (FLAG-P9-CRIT-003/callers-1).
- **Wrap the author-test replay loop in environ snapshot/restore** so pytest doesn't leak task-run variables across tests (FLAG-P9-003/correctness-3).
- **Delete/rewrite `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`** — its 4 scaffold tests assert the old exit-2 behavior and will break when `_cmd_test` is rewritten (FLAG-P9-001/scope-3/all_locations-4).
- **Support `--pack` / `--orchestrator` / `--fixture` CLI flags** as the brief explicitly requires for the stop condition (issue_hints-2/issue_hints-3). Keep the existing positional `qualified_id` as a convenience alias.
- **Add `source: author_test` as a top-level event field on attested events** but **exclude it from `canonical_event_json`** so hash-chains stay valid (FLAG-P9-002/issue_hints-4/all_locations-1/callers-3).
- **Only skip identity validation** in auto-approval — keep `match_attested_command` intact (correctness-2).
- **The live `events.jsonl` always retains hashes**; only the persisted golden is normalized (correctness-4).
- **Auto-approval uses `step.ack.kind`** for attestor_kind, not hard-coded "agent" (correctness-5).
- **Document `repeat.until.user_approves` behavior under auto-approval** (correctness-6).
- **Note the asymmetric guard in `lifecycle_ack`** — fine for Phase 9 since the test loop calls `gate_command` directly (scope-4).
- **Update pipeline entrypoint help text** (all_locations-2).

## Phase 1: Foundation — DSL Orchestrator & Infrastructure

### Step 1: Create `author_test_smoke.py` DSL orchestrator (`artagents/packs/builtin/author_test_smoke.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** a minimal DSL orchestrator at `artagents/packs/builtin/author_test_smoke.py` with plan_id `builtin.author_test_smoke`. Include TWO steps: one `code` step that writes a file (so produces checks pass) and one `attested` step with `ack.kind=agent` so auto-approval is exercised by the golden run:

```python
from artagents.orchestrate import attested, code, file_nonempty, plan

plan("builtin.author_test_smoke", [
    code("gen", argv=["python3", "-c",
        "import json, os; "
        "path = os.path.join(os.environ['ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_DIR'], 'out.json'); "
        "json.dump({'ok': True}, open(path, 'w'))"],
        produces={"out.json": file_nonempty()}),
    attested("review", instructions="reviewed by author_test",
             command="artagents ack review --decision approve",
             ack="agent"),
])
```

**Design decisions:**
- The `code` step writes `out.json` to `$ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_DIR` which equals `step_dir`, so `artifact_path = step_dir / "out.json"` resolves correctly.
- The `attested` step uses `ack="agent"` (the string shorthand). Under auto-approval this synthesizes `attestor_kind="agent"` which matches.
- `plan_id` is `builtin.author_test_smoke` — no collision with the legacy `builtin.hype` (`orchestrator.yaml` or `hype/` package).

2. **Compile** it: `artagents author compile builtin.author_test_smoke` → writes `build/author_test_smoke.json` (and creates `build/` dir as needed).

**Note**: The `build/` path is `artagents/packs/builtin/build/author_test_smoke.json` per `compile_to_path:142` (`root / pack / "build" / f"{name}.json"` where `root = packs_root`, `pack = "builtin"`). This path is gitignored by the existing `artagents/packs/*/build/` rule — the compiled JSON won't be accidentally committed, but the author-test `--pack` resolution will find it at runtime.

### Step 2: Create fixture and golden directories
**Scope:** Small

1. The existing CLI resolves paths as:
   - `golden_path = pack_root / "golden" / f"{fixture_name}.events.jsonl"` (cli.py:330)
   - `fixture_dir = pack_root / "fixtures" / fixture_name` (cli.py:331)
   - where `pack_root = root / pack` (cli.py:329), so `pack_root = packs/builtin/`

2. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/` — empty directory with `.keep` marker.
3. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/golden/` — empty directory with `.keep` marker. The `smoke.events.jsonl` will be generated on first `--regenerate` run.

**Gitignore check**: The `.gitignore` has no rule blocking `fixtures/` or `golden/` at pack level — these directories will be trackable.

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode for Attested Steps

### Step 3: Add auto-approval in gate dispatch (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add** a helper at the top of `gate.py`:
```python
def _is_author_test_mode() -> bool:
    return os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST", "") == "1"
```

2. **Modify `_dispatch_attested`** (line 904): After `match_attested_command` succeeds (DO NOT skip it — correctness-2), check `_is_author_test_mode()`:
   - If True: skip `validate_attested_identity`. Instead, synthesize:
     - `attestor_kind = step.ack.kind` (correctness-5)
     - `attestor_id = "author_test"`
     - `evidence = ("source:author_test",) + args.evidence`
     - Record a synthetic `step_attested` event with `"source": "author_test"` as a top-level key.
   - If False: keep the existing `validate_attested_identity` path.

3. **The `source: author_test` field** on the event is included in the event dict but must NOT appear in `canonical_event_json`. Update `canonical_event_json` (events.py:20-22) to also strip `"source"` alongside `"hash"`:
```python
def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key not in ("hash", "source")}
    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
```

4. **Document** repeat.until.user_approves semantics (correctness-6): In auto-approval mode, any attested step inside a `repeat.until(condition="user_approves")` will auto-approve on iteration 1, satisfying the until condition, so the loop terminates immediately. Goldens will only contain `iteration=1` events. This is by design — the author must deliberately run non-auto-approval to test multi-iteration paths. Add this as a comment in `_dispatch_attested`.

### Step 4: Scrub `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` from child subprocess env (`artagents/core/task/env.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` to env.py constants.

2. **Modify `child_subprocess_env`** (line 68): Add `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` to the purge list alongside `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`:
```python
env.pop(ARTAGENTS_ACTOR, None)
env.pop("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST", None)
```

This ensures that child processes spawned during task runs (code steps, executors) can NEVER inherit auto-approval mode.

## Phase 3: Event Normalization

### Step 5: Create normalization helper (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, *, run_dir: str) -> dict` that:
   - Strips volatile fields: `"ts"`, `"hash"`, `"run_id"`, `"pid"`, `"source"`.
   - Rewrites absolute paths: replaces the literal run-dir prefix (passed as `run_dir`) with `<RUN_DIR>/`.
   - Keeps structural fields: `"kind"`, `"plan_step_id"`, `"plan_step_path"`, `"command"`, `"returncode"`, `"check_id"`, `"produces_name"`, `"cas_sha256"`, `"evidence"`, `"attestor_kind"`, `"attestor_id"`, `"iteration"`, `"item_id"`, `"child_plan_hash"`, `"reason"`, etc.
   - Sorts keys for deterministic output.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_file(path: Path, *, run_dir: str) -> list[dict]` that reads JSONL, normalizes each line, and returns the list.

3. **Note**: Normalization is only applied to the **copy for diffing** and the **persisted golden** on `--regenerate`. The live `events.jsonl` on disk is never modified — it must retain hashes for `verify_chain`.

### Step 6: Update `__init__.py` exports (`artagents/core/task/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. Export `normalize_event` and `normalize_events_file` from the core/task public API.

## Phase 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` with Runtime Replay

### Step 7: Rewrite `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Large

1. **Replace** the existing `_cmd_test` (lines 311–371) with the full runtime replay. The function signature changes:

```python
def _cmd_test(
    *,
    pack_id: str,
    orch_id: str,
    fixture_name: str,
    packs_root: Path,
    regenerate: bool = False,
) -> int:
```

2. **Runtime replay loop**:

   a. **Locate the compiled plan**: `pack_root / "build" / f"{orch_id}.json"`. If missing, compile-on-the-fly via `compile_to_path(f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", packs_root=packs_root)`.

   b. **Create** a scratch project dir via `tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()`. Use this as the `projects_root`.

   c. **Setup**: Copy the fixture artifacts (if any — for smoke test, none needed) into the scratch dir. Then invoke `cmd_start` programmatically to initialize the project (writes `plan.json`, `active_run.json`, `runs/<run_id>/`, and the `run_started` event).

   d. **Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`** in `os.environ` BEFORE entering the loop, and save a snapshot of the relevant env vars:
     ```python
     _TASK_RUN_ENV_KEYS = ("ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID", "ARTAGENTS_TASK_PROJECT", "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID", "ARTAGENTS_TASK_ITEM_ID", "ARTAGENTS_TASK_ITERATION")
     saved = {}
     for key in _TASK_RUN_ENV_KEYS:
         saved[key] = os.environ.get(key)
     os.environ["ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"] = "1"
     try:
         # ... replay loop ...
     finally:
         for key in _TASK_RUN_ENV_KEYS:
             if key in saved:
                 os.environ[key] = saved[key]
             else:
                 os.environ.pop(key, None)
         os.environ.pop("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST", None)
     ```

   e. **Loop**: For each step:
      - Call `peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id)`.
      - If `peek.exhausted`: break.
      - For `CodeStep`: call `gate_command(slug, command, argv, root=scratch_dir)`. Run the subprocess via `subprocess.run(argv, cwd=step_dir)`. Call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, returncode)`.
      - For `AttestedStep`: call `gate_command(slug, command, full_command, root=scratch_dir)` where `full_command` is the attested command with synthetic `--agent author_test` token. The gate's `_dispatch_attested` in auto-approval mode handles the rest.
      - Reload the updated events after each step by re-reading `events.jsonl`.

   f. **After loop**: Load the completed `events.jsonl` from the scratch run_dir.

   g. **Normalize** events using `normalize_events_file` with the scratch run_dir as the `run_dir` for path rewriting.

3. **Diff/regenerate**:
   - If `--regenerate`: write the normalized events as the golden file. Print: `"wrote <path> (commit this golden if the change is intentional)"`.
   - Otherwise: load and normalize the golden. Diff via `difflib.unified_diff`. Return 0 on match, 1 on drift.

4. **Delete** the old scaffold branches (lines 334–348 with the exit-2 and "implement Phase 9" messages).

### Step 8: Wire `--pack` / `--orchestrator` / `--fixture` / `--regenerate` into CLI
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Update `_build_parser`** (line 462):
   - Keep the positional `qualified_id` as an optional convenience.
   - Add `--pack` / `--orchestrator` / `--fixture` / `--regenerate` flags.
   - Allow either form: positional `qualified_id` OR `--pack` + `--orchestrator`. Both are accepted; if both given, `--pack`/`--orchestrator` wins.

```python
test_p = sub.add_parser("test", help="author test [<pack>.<name>] --fixture <name>")
test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", nargs="?", default=None, help="qualified id <pack>.<name>")
test_p.add_argument("--pack", default=None, help="pack id")
test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", default=None, help="orchestrator name")
test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, help="fixture name")
test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", default=False, help="regenerate golden from current events")
```

2. **Update `main`** (line 474): Resolve `--pack`/`--orchestrator` vs positional, then call `_cmd_test` with the resolved values.

3. **Add `--regenerate`** flag description to `_print_entrypoint_help` in `artagents/pipeline.py` line 199:
```
python3 -m artagents author test [<pack>.<name>] --fixture <name> [--regenerate]
```

## Phase 5: Tests

### Step 9: Delete scaffold tests (`tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Delete** the entire file `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`. Its 4 tests assert old scaffold semantics (exit 2 with "implement Phase 9" message) that no longer exist after the rewrite.
2. The new tests (Steps 10-13) cover equivalent functionality with runtime replay semantics.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Test** that `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator author_test_smoke --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file. The test:
   - Compiles `builtin.author_test_smoke`.
   - Runs `--regenerate` first to create the initial golden.
   - Runs the test again without `--regenerate` and asserts exit code 0.

### Step 11: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Test** that corrupting the golden causes exit 1 with unified diff. Approach:
   - Set up a clean golden via `--regenerate`.
   - Corrupt the golden by changing a `kind` field (e.g., `"step_attested"` → `"step_attested_BROKEN"`).
   - Run `author test` and assert exit 1, stdout contains `--- ` and the corrupted value.

### Step 12: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Test** that `--regenerate` rewrites the golden file:
   - Start with an empty golden.
   - Run with `--regenerate`.
   - Assert the golden file exists and contains valid JSONL events.
   - Run again without `--regenerate` and assert exit 0 (golden matches current).

### Step 13: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Test** auto-approval behavior:
   a. **With ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1**: Create a run with an AttestedStep, call `gate_command` with the attested command (synthetic agent token), assert the attested event is emitted and the cursor advances.
   b. **Without ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1**: Same setup, assert the gate rejects with "requires --agent or --actor".
   c. **child_subprocess_env scrubs AUTHOR_TEST**: Assert `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` is absent from the dict returned by `child_subprocess_env()` even when set in `os.environ`.
   d. **canonical_event_json excludes source**: Assert that an event with `{"kind": "step_attested", "source": "author_test", "hash": "sha256:..."}` produces canonical JSON without `"source"` or `"hash"`.

## Phase 6: Validate & Polish

### Step 14: Run full test suite
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `pytest tests/ -x` — all existing tests must continue to pass.
2. **Run** new tests: `pytest tests/test_author_test_*.py -v`.
3. **Run** CLI smoke: `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator author_test_smoke --fixture smoke` exits 0.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (author_test_smoke.py DSL) → Step 2 (fixture/golden dirs)
2. Step 3 (auto-approval in gate.py) → Step 4 (scrub env var)
3. Step 5 (normalization helper) → Step 6 (exports)
4. Step 7 (rewrite _cmd_test) → Step 8 (CLI flags)
5. Step 9 (delete scaffold tests)
6. Step 10–13 (new tests)
7. Step 14 (full validation)

## Key Design Decisions (Revised)

1. **New pack name `builtin.author_test_smoke`**: Avoids same-stem collision with `hype/` package, double-id collision, and the FLAG-003 guard. The stop condition command becomes `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator author_test_smoke --fixture smoke`.

2. **Two-step smoke fixture** (code + attested): Exercises both CodeStep dispatch and AttestedStep auto-approval in the canonical golden run. The code step writes a file that passes `file_nonempty`.

3. **`source: author_test` excluded from canonical_event_json**: Keeps hash-chains valid without altering the event schema too deeply. The `source` field is metadata for the diff/golden, not part of the chain.

4. **Only identity validation skipped**: `match_attested_command` remains intact — it's the command-to-cursor coupling that `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` depends on.

5. **Live events.jsonl never normalized**: Only the diff comparison and the persisted golden use normalized forms.

6. **Environ snapshot/restore**: The author test loop wraps the entire operation in try/finally that saves/restores task-run env vars.

7. **Scrub `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` in `child_subprocess_env`**: Production subprocesses can never see the auto-approval flag.

8. **Asymmetric guard in lifecycle_ack noted**: Phase 9 test loop calls `gate_command` directly, so the lifecycle_ack path is not invoked. Documented as a known asymmetry for future phases.

1	"""Hash-chained task-run event log."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import errno
6	import hashlib
7	import json
8	import os
9	from datetime import UTC, datetime
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	ZERO_HASH = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
14	
15	
16	class EventLogError(RuntimeError):
17	    """Raised when a task event log cannot be read or written."""
18	
19	
20	def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
21	    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key != "hash"}
22	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
23	
24	
25	def append_event(path: str | Path, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
26	    events_path = Path(path)
27	    ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)
28	    if not ok:
29	        raise EventLogError(error or f"invalid event hash chain in {events_path}")
30	
31	    previous_events = read_events(events_path)
32	    prev_hash = previous_events[-1]["hash"] if previous_events else ZERO_HASH
33	
34	    stored = dict(event)
35	    stored.pop("hash", None)
36	    stored["hash"] = _event_hash(prev_hash, stored)
37	
38	    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
39	    created = not events_path.exists()
40	    try:
41	        with events_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
42	            handle.write(json.dumps(stored, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False))
43	            handle.write("\n")
44	            handle.flush()
45	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
46	        if created:
47	            _fsync_dir(events_path.parent)
48	    except OSError as exc:
49	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to append event to {events_path}: {exc}") from exc
50	    return stored
51	
52	
53	def verify_chain(path: str | Path) -> tuple[bool, int, str | None]:
54	    events_path = Path(path)
55	    try:
56	        handle = events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8")
57	    except FileNotFoundError:
58	        return True, -1, None
59	    except OSError as exc:
60	        return False, -1, f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}"
61	
62	    prev_hash = ZERO_HASH
63	    last_index = -1
64	    with handle:
65	        for index, line in enumerate(handle):
66	            if not line.endswith("\n"):
67	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not newline-terminated"
68	            raw = line[:-1]
69	            if not raw:
70	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is empty"
71	            try:
72	                event = json.loads(raw)
73	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
74	                return False, index, f"invalid JSON on event log line {index + 1}: {exc.msg}"
75	            if not isinstance(event, dict):
76	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not an object"
77	            stored_hash = event.get("hash")
78	            if not isinstance(stored_hash, str):
79	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is missing hash"
80	            expected_hash = _event_hash(prev_hash, event)
81	            if stored_hash != expected_hash:
82	                return (
83	                    False,
84	                    index,
85	                    f"event log line {index + 1} hash mismatch: expected {expected_hash}, got {stored_hash}",
86	                )
87	            prev_hash = stored_hash
88	            last_index = index
89	    return True, last_index, None
90	
91	
92	def read_events(path: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
93	    events_path = Path(path)
94	    try:
95	        with events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
96	            return [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
97	    except FileNotFoundError:
98	        return []
99	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
100	        raise EventLogError(f"invalid JSON in {events_path}: {exc.msg}") from exc

367	def _validate_produces(raw: Any, index: int, *, allow_legacy_list: bool) -> tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]:

369	        return ()
370	    if isinstance(raw, list):
371	        if not allow_legacy_list:

374	            )
375	        if not raw:
376	            return ()

378	        seen: set[str] = set()
379	        for item in raw:

381	                raise TaskPlanError(
382	                    f"plan steps[{index}].produces legacy list must contain non-empty strings"
383	                )

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 3)

## Overview

Phase 9 transforms the existing Phase 5 scaffold `_cmd_test` (which diffs pre-existing golden/fixture events files) into a full runtime replay: run an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture scratch dir with auto-approval ON, capture the live `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against the golden. The `--regenerate` flag writes the current (normalized) events as the new golden.

**Resolution of the orchestrator-name divergence (FLAG-P9-006 / issue_hints-1):** The brief's STOP CONDITION literally requires `--orchestrator hype` and fixtures at `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/...`. We honor this directly. The new DSL orchestrator is placed at `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` (a `.py` file alongside the existing `builtin/hype/` package). `resolve_orchestrator` in `compile.py` uses `spec_from_file_location`, so loading `builtin/hype.py` as an isolated module is benign — it doesn't pollute the `builtin.hype` package namespace, doesn't interfere with `from artagents.packs.builtin.hype import run`, and doesn't trigger FLAG-003 (which is a `_cmd_new` guard, not a `resolve_orchestrator` restriction). Fixtures and golden land under `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` and `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` exactly as the brief Item 5 specifies.

**Constraints applied from critique:**
- **Name the DSL file `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py`** with `plan_id = "builtin.hype"`. The `hype.py` file coexists with the `hype/` package; `spec_from_file_location` isolates the load. The `_cmd_new` FLAG-003 guard is a scaffolding-time check and doesn't apply here.
- **Drop the `source` field entirely** (FLAG-P9-002, correctness-3, scope-1). Instead of a generic `source` field on attested events that risks future schema collisions and requires modifying `canonical_event_json`, simply do NOT add any extra top-level field. Auto-approval synthesizes the attestation directly. The auto-approval marker lives in `evidence` as `"source:author_test"`. No changes to `canonical_event_json` are needed.
- **The code step writes to a relative path** (FLAG-P9-CRIT-001, issue_hints-2, correctness-1). The argv uses `open('out.json','w')` and the test loop sets `cwd=step_dir`. No reliance on nonexistent env vars.
- **Every subprocess.run in the test loop passes `env=child_subprocess_env()`** (FLAG-P9-010, callers-2). Additionally, executor runner `runner.py:308-312` is updated to scrub `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` from its env.
- **Wrap the test loop in environ snapshot/restore** (FLAG-P9-003).
- **Delete `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`** (FLAG-P9-001).
- **Support `--pack` / `--orchestrator` / `--fixture` / `--regenerate` CLI flags** with explicit validation when neither form is given (all_locations-2).
- **Only skip identity validation** — keep `match_attested_command` intact (correctness-2).
- **Live events.jsonl always retains hashes**; only golden is normalized.
- **Auto-approval uses `step.ack.kind`** for attestor_kind (correctness-5).
- **Test loop constructs correct `--agent` / `--actor` token** per `step.ack.kind` (callers-4).
- **Document `repeat.until.user_approves` under auto-approval** (correctness-6).
- **Update pipeline entrypoint help** (all_locations-2).
- **Update `docs/AUTHORING.md`** (all_locations-4).
- **`test_author_test_pass.py` loads committed golden, never regenerates** (FLAG-P9-009, correctness-6).
- **Tests use `monkeypatch.delenv`** (correctness-5).
- **Use `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` constant** from env.py (correctness-7).

## Phase 1: Foundation — DSL Orchestrator & Infrastructure

### Step 1: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` DSL orchestrator
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` with `plan_id = "builtin.hype"`. Include TWO steps: one `code` that writes a file, one `attested` with `ack.kind="agent"`:

```python
from artagents.orchestrate import attested, code, file_nonempty, plan

plan("builtin.hype", [
    code("gen", argv=["python3", "-c",
        "import json; json.dump({'ok': True}, open('out.json','w'))"
    ], produces={"out.json": file_nonempty()}),
    attested("review", instructions="reviewed by author_test",
             command="artagents ack review --decision approve",
             ack="agent"),
])
```

**Design decisions:**
- The `code` step writes `out.json` as a relative path. The test loop sets `cwd=step_dir` (via `step_dir_for_path`), so the file lands at `step_dir / "out.json"` which is what `_run_inline_checks` expects.
- The `attested` step uses `ack="agent"`. Under auto-approval the test loop constructs `argv = ["artagents", "ack", "review", "--decision", "approve", "--agent", "author_test"]`. `match_attested_command` strips `--agent author_test` and compares the remainder to `step.command`, matching.
- `plan_id` = `builtin.hype` — exactly what the brief's STOP CONDITION demands.
- `hype.py` alongside `hype/`. `resolve_orchestrator` loads via `spec_from_file_location` with a unique module name, fully isolated from the `builtin.hype` package. Not affected by FLAG-003 (that's `_cmd_new`-only).

2. **Compile**: `artagents author compile builtin.hype` → `build/hype.json`.

### Step 2: Create fixture and golden directories
**Scope:** Small

1. Use fixture name `hype/smoke` to encode the orchestrator within the pack-scoped scheme:
   - `golden_path = builtin/golden/hype/smoke.events.jsonl`
   - `fixture_dir = builtin/fixtures/hype/smoke/`

2. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/hype/smoke/` with `.keep`.
3. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/golden/hype/` directory.

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode for Attested Steps

### Step 3: Add auto-approval in `_dispatch_attested` (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add** helper at top of `gate.py`:
```python
from artagents.core.task.env import AUTHOR_TEST_ENV

def _is_author_test_mode() -> bool:
    return os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV, "") == "1"
```

2. **Modify `_dispatch_attested`** (line 904): After `match_attested_command` succeeds, check `_is_author_test_mode()`:
   - If True: skip `validate_attested_identity`. Set `attestor_kind = step.ack.kind`, `attestor_id = "author_test"`, `evidence = ("source:author_test",) + args.evidence`.dispatch Proceed to emit the attested event using these synthetic values.
   - If False: keep existing `validate_attested_identity` path.

3. **No new top-level field on events.** The auto-approval marker lives only in `evidence` (part of the event payload, already normalized for diffs). `canonical_event_json` is unchanged.

4. **Document `repeat.until.user_approves`**: In auto-approval mode, a `repeat.until(condition="user_approves")` attested step auto-approves on iteration 1, satisfying the until condition, so the loop terminates immediately. Goldens contain only `iteration=1`. Authors must run non-auto-approval to test multi-iteration paths. Add as block comment in `_dispatch_attested`.

### Step 4: Add `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` constant and scrub from child env (`artagents/core/task/env.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** constant at module level:
```python
AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"
```

2. **Modify `child_subprocess_env`** (line 68): Add to purge list:
```python
env.pop(ARTAGENTS_ACTOR, None)
env.pop(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV, None)
```

3. **Fix executor runner** (`artagents/core/executor/runner.py:308-312`):
```python
full_env = {**os.environ, **env, **_project_subprocess_env(request), **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()}
full_env.pop("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST", None)
completed = subprocess.run(list(command), cwd=cwd, env=full_env, check=False)
```

## Phase 3: Event Normalization

### Step 5: Create normalization helper (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, *, run_dir: str) -> dict`:
   - Strips `"ts"`, `"hash"`, `"run_id"`, `"pid"`.
   - Rewrites absolute paths: replaces literal run-dir prefix with `<RUN_DIR>/`.
   - Keeps all other structural fields.
   - Sorts keys.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_file(path: Path, *, run_dir: str) -> list[dict]`.

3. Applied only to the diff copy and the persisted golden. Live `events.jsonl` untouched.

### Step 6: Import directly — no `__init__.py` export
**Scope:** No-op — just a note: import as `from artagents.core.task.normalize import normalize_events_file` in cli.py.

## Phase 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` with Runtime Replay

### Step 7: Rewrite `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Large

1. **New signature**:
```python
def _cmd_test(
    *,
    pack_id: str,
    orch_id: str,
    fixture_name: str,
    packs_root: Path,
    regenerate: bool = False,
) -> int:
```

2. **Runtime replay loop**:
   a. **Compile first**: Always `compile_to_path(f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", packs_root=packs_root)`.
   b. **Locate**: `pack_root / "build" / f"{orch_id}.json"`.
   c. **Scratch dir**: `tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()` as `projects_root`.
   d. **Start**: Programmatic `cmd_start(argv)` where `argv = [f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", "--project", slug]`.
   e. **Environ snapshot/restore**: Save task env vars + AUTHOR_TEST, set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, restore in `finally`.
   f. **Loop**: `peek_current_step` → for CodeStep: `shlex.split(step.command)`, `subprocess.run(argv, cwd=step_dir, env=child_subprocess_env())`, `record_dispatch_complete`. For AttestedStep: `shlex.split(step.command)` + `--agent author_test` (or `--actor` per `step.ack.kind`), call `gate_command(slug, step.command, full_argv, root=scratch_dir)`. Reload events each iteration.
   g. **Normalize**: After loop, `normalize_events_file` on completed events.
   h. **Diff/regenerate**: `--regenerate` → write golden. Else → `difflib.unified_diff`, return 0/1.

3. **Delete** old scaffold body (missing/empty golden → exit 2, file-diff code).

### Step 8: Wire CLI flags into parser and `main` (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Update `_build_parser`**:
```python
test_p = sub.add_parser("test", help="author test [<pack>.<name>] --fixture <name> [--regenerate]")
test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", nargs="?", default=None)
test_p.add_argument("--pack", default=None)
test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", default=None)
test_p.add_argument("--fixture", default=None)
test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", default=False)
```

2. **Update `main`**: If `--pack`/`--orchestrator` given, use them. Else if positional given, `_qualified_split`. Else print `error: --pack and --orchestrator are required when qualified id is not provided` and exit 2.

3. **Update `pipeline.py` entrypoint help** line 199: add `[--regenerate]`.

### Step 9: Update `docs/AUTHORING.md`
**Scope:** Small

1. Add section documenting `author test` semantics, `--regenerate`, auto-approval mode, and `repeat.until.user_approves` behavior.

## Phase 5: Fixture & Golden

### Step 10: Generate and commit the initial golden
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run**: `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture hype/smoke --regenerate`
2. **Verify** golden events: `run_started`, `gen` dispatch/complete, produces check passed, `review` step_attested with `attestor_id="author_test"`.
3. **Commit** the golden file.

## Phase 6: Tests

### Step 11: Delete `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`
**Scope:** Small — 4 tests asserting old exit-2/Phase-9-message behavior no longer exist.

### Step 12: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Medium — Compiles `builtin.hype`, copies committed golden into temp packs dir, runs `author test` without `--regenerate`, asserts exit 0. Does NOT regenerate.

### Step 13: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small — Corrupt golden, assert exit 1 + unified diff.

### Step 14: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small — Empty golden → `--regenerate` → golden exists → test without `--regenerate` → exit 0.

### Step 15: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium — Tests: (a) with AUTHOR_TEST=1 → auto-approve; (b) via `monkeypatch.delenv` → reject; (c) `child_subprocess_env()` scrubs AUTHOR_TEST; (d) `canonical_event_json` unchanged; (e) `ack.kind=actor` → `--actor author_test` token works.

## Phase 7: Validate

### Step 16: Run full test suite
**Scope:** Small — `pytest tests/ -x`, `pytest tests/test_author_test_*.py -v`, CLI smoke: `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture hype/smoke` exits 0.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 + Step 2 (orchestrator + dirs)
2. Step 4 (env constant + scrubbing)
3. Step 3 (auto-approval, depends on Step 4 constant)
4. Step 5 (normalization)
5. Steps 7 + 8 (cli rewrite + flags)
6. Step 9 (docs)
7. Step 10 (generate + commit golden)
8. Step 11 (delete scaffold tests)
9. Steps 12–15 (new tests)
10. Step 16 (full validation)

## Key Design Decisions (Revision 3)

1. **Orchestrator name `hype`**: DSL file at `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` alongside `hype/` package. Loaded via `spec_from_file_location` in isolation. Brief's STOP CONDITION command fully executable.
2. **No `source` field**: Auto-approval records `"source:author_test"` in `evidence`. No changes to `canonical_event_json`.
3. **Two-step smoke fixture** exercises both CodeStep and auto-approval.
4. **Only identity validation skipped**; `match_attested_command` preserved.
5. **Every test-loop subprocess uses `child_subprocess_env()`**; executor runner also scrubbed.
6. **Environ snapshot/restore** around test loop.
7. **Fixture name `hype/smoke`** avoids pack-level collision.
8. **`test_author_test_pass.py` loads committed golden** — never regenerates.
9. **Scalability note**: legacy `hype/run.py` uses `os.environ` directly in subprocess calls — out of scope. Author-test loop code steps are protected.

  "questions": [
    "Should the fixture name be `smoke` (simple, single word) with the understanding that it lives at pack level, meaning any future orchestrator in the `builtin` pack named `smoke` would collide? Or should we use `hype/smoke` to namespace it by orchestrator? The plan currently uses `hype/smoke` as a compromise that keeps the pack-scoped path scheme while avoiding collision. The brief's Item 5 says `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` which implies orch-scoped, but the existing CLI scheme is pack-scoped. The `hype/smoke` encoding works within the pack-scoped scheme. Is this acceptable?",
    "The brief Item 5 says smoke fixture should include 'minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub)'. For a pure write-file smoke test, inputs are irrelevant. Should we include a stub file anyway to exercise the fixture-copy logic, or leave it as `.keep`-only since copying doesn't affect the events?"
  ],

    "The new DSL orchestrator lives at `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` with plan_id `builtin.hype`. `spec_from_file_location` in compile.py loads it in complete isolation from the `hype/` package \u2014 no import shadowing, no sys.modules collision, no runtime interference with the legacy `from artagents.packs.builtin.hype import run` callers. The `_cmd_new` FLAG-003 guard only applies to scaffolding and does not constrain `resolve_orchestrator`.",
    "The code step writes `out.json` as a relative path. The test loop computes `step_dir` via `step_dir_for_path` and passes it as `cwd` to `subprocess.run`. The file lands at `step_dir/out.json`, which is exactly where `_run_inline_checks` looks for it.",

    "The executor runner (`runner.py:308-312`) build of `{**os.environ, ...}` is the only production subprocess site that bypasses `child_subprocess_env()`. Its fix (popping AUTHOR_TEST from the final env dict) closes the leakage path for code steps that trigger executor runs.",

115	def step_dir_for_path(
116	    slug: str,
117	    run_id: str,
118	    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...],
119	    *,
120	    iteration: int | None = None,
121	    item_id: str | None = None,
122	    root: str | Path | None = None,
123	) -> Path:

125	    validate_run_id(run_id)
126	    if not plan_step_path:
127	        raise TaskPlanError("plan_step_path must contain at least one segment")
128	    for segment in plan_step_path:
129	        validate_run_id(segment)
130	    if iteration is not None and item_id is not None:
131	        raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: iteration and item_id are mutually exclusive")
132	    base = project_dir(slug, root=root) / "runs" / run_id / "steps"
133	    for segment in plan_step_path:
134	        base = base / segment
135	    if iteration is not None:
136	        if not isinstance(iteration, int) or isinstance(iteration, bool) or iteration < 1:
137	            raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: iteration must be an int >= 1")
138	        base = base / "iterations" / f"{iteration:03d}"
139	    elif item_id is not None:
140	        validate_run_id(item_id)
141	        base = base / "items" / item_id
142	    return base

155	def iter_steps_with_path(plan: TaskPlan) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], TaskPlanStep]]:
156	    """Yield ``(path, step)`` in pre-order for every step in the plan tree."""
157	
158	    def _walk(steps: tuple[TaskPlanStep, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], TaskPlanStep]]:
159	        for step in steps:
160	            path = prefix + (step.id,)
161	            yield path, step
162	            if isinstance(step, NestedStep):
163	                yield from _walk(step.plan.steps, path)

Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).
