PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 4)

## Overview

Phase 9 replaces the Phase 5 scaffold `_cmd_test` (which diffs pre-existing fixture vs golden events files) with a full runtime replay: compile the orchestrator, run the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON, capture live `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against the committed golden. The `--regenerate` flag writes the current (normalized) events as the new golden.

**Fixture/golden path layout** now matches the brief's literal spec (FLAG-P9-006, issue_hints-1, issue_hints-2, scope-2): fixtures live at `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and goldens at `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`. The STOP CONDITION command `--pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` executes verbatim.

**Auto-approval** lives in `_dispatch_attested` (gate.py). When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, identity validation is skipped, attestor_kind comes from `step.ack.kind`, and attestor_id is `"author_test"`. The auto-approval marker goes into `evidence` as `"source:author_test"` — no changes to `canonical_event_json`.

**The test loop** calls `gate_command` directly for attested steps (not the CLI `ack` path), constructing the command string with `--agent author_test` (or `--actor` per `step.ack.kind`) so `match_attested_command` sees and strips the token before the auto-approval branch fires. Every `subprocess.run` passes `env=child_subprocess_env()` and the executor runner also scrubs `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`.

**Constraints applied from critique:**
- Fixture/golden paths: `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Test loop constructs command string with `--agent`/`--actor` appended so `match_attested_command` works.
- `step_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` before each `subprocess.run(cwd=step_dir)`.
- `cmd_start` called with explicit `packs_root` and `projects_root` kwargs; `run_id` read from `active_run.json`.
- `--fixture` is validated as required in `main()`.
- `docs/AUTHORING.md` is CREATED (not updated — it doesn't exist yet).
- Fixture-copy step: `shutil.copytree(fixture_dir, scratch_slug_dir, dirs_exist_ok=True)` before `cmd_start`.
- Executor runner scrub: only line 311 dict-merge site needs fixing (lines 304 and 319 are result-object field assignments, not subprocess sites).
- `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` constant from `env.py` used everywhere.

## Phase 1: Foundation — DSL Orchestrator & Fixture Directories

### Step 1: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` DSL orchestrator
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` with `plan_id = "builtin.hype"`. Two steps: one `code` that writes a JSON file, one `attested` with `ack.kind="agent"`:

```python
from artagents.orchestrate import attested, code, file_nonempty, plan

plan("builtin.hype", [
    code("gen", argv=["python3", "-c",
        "import json; json.dump({'ok': True}, open('out.json','w'))"
    ], produces={"out.json": file_nonempty()}),
    attested("review", instructions="reviewed by author_test",
             command="artagents ack review --decision approve",
             ack="agent"),
])
```

**Design:**
- `code` step writes `out.json` as a relative path. The test loop sets `cwd=step_dir` so the file lands at `step_dir/out.json`, which `_run_inline_checks` expects.
- `attested` step uses `ack="agent"`. Under auto-approval, the test loop constructs `command_str = "artagents ack review --decision approve --agent author_test"` and calls `gate_command`. `match_attested_command` strips `--agent author_test`, compares remainder to `step.command`, matches.
- `plan_id = "builtin.hype"` — exactly what the brief's STOP CONDITION requires.
- `hype.py` coexists with `hype/` package. `resolve_orchestrator` loads via `spec_from_file_location` with a unique module name, fully isolated from the `builtin.hype` package. FLAG-003 is `_cmd_new`-only.

2. **Compile**: `artagents author compile builtin.hype` → `build/hype.json`.

### Step 2: Create fixture and golden directories (orch-scoped)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** directories per the brief's literal layout (Item 5 + STOP CONDITION):
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` with `.keep` marker.
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/` directory (empty until golden is generated).

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode + Env Scrubbing

### Step 3: Add `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` constant and scrub from child env (`artagents/core/task/env.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** constant at module level (after line 13):
```python
AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"
```

2. **Modify `child_subprocess_env`** (line 68): Add to the pop list:
```python
env.pop(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV, None)
```

3. **Fix executor runner** (`artagents/core/executor/runner.py:311`): After the dict merge, pop the env var:
```python
env={**os.environ, **env, **_project_subprocess_env(request), **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()},
```
becomes:
```python
full_env = {**os.environ, **env, **_project_subprocess_env(request), **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()}
full_env.pop("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST", None)
...
completed = subprocess.run(list(command), cwd=cwd, env=full_env, check=False)
```

**Audit notes** (correctness-3, scope-3 resolved):
- `runner.py:304` (`env=env`): dry-run path, returns a result object — no subprocess executed.
- `runner.py:319` (`env=env`): result object field assignment — no subprocess executed.
- `runner.py:311` (dict-merge + subprocess.run): the ONLY production subprocess site — fixed above.
- Legacy `hype/run.py` subprocess sites with bare `os.environ`: out of scope for Phase 9 (known limitation, affects only the legacy orchestrator, not the new DSL one).

### Step 4: Add auto-approval in `_dispatch_attested` (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add** import at top of `gate.py`:
```python
from artagents.core.task.env import AUTHOR_TEST_ENV

def _is_author_test_mode() -> bool:
    return os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV, "") == "1"
```
(Add `import os` if not already imported.)

2. **Modify `_dispatch_attested`** (after line 920, the `match_attested_command` call):

Insert after `matched, args = match_attested_command(command, step.command)`:
```python
    if not matched:
        _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)

    if _is_author_test_mode():
        # Auto-approval: skip identity validation, synthesize attestation.
        # repeat.until(condition="user_approves") attested steps auto-approve
        # on iteration 1, satisfying the until condition, so the loop terminates
        # immediately. Goldens contain only iteration=1. Authors must run
        # non-auto-approval to test multi-iteration paths.
        attestor_kind = step.ack.kind
        attestor_id = "author_test"
        evidence = ("source:author_test",) + args.evidence
    else:
        attestor_kind, attestor_id = validate_attested_identity(
            slug=slug,
            step=step,
            args=args,
            run_started_actor=run_started_actor,
        )
        evidence = args.evidence
```

Then update the event-emitting code (lines 929-943) to use `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, and `evidence` from above instead of calling `validate_attested_identity` inline:
```python
    if item_id is not None:
        append_event(
            events_path,
            make_item_attested_event(
                path_tuple,
                item_id,
                attestor_kind=attestor_kind,
                attestor_id=attestor_id,
                evidence=evidence,
            ),
        )
    else:
        append_event(
            events_path,
            make_step_attested_event(path_str, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence),
        )
```

**Design decision:** The auto-approval marker `"source:author_test"` lives in `evidence`, which is already part of the event payload and appears in normalized diffs. No changes to `canonical_event_json`. The `source` attribute on events does not exist — no schema collision risk.

**Known limitation (scope-1):** `lifecycle_ack._ack_approve` does not honor `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`. But the test loop calls `gate_command` directly, never the CLI `ack` path. Under author-test mode, no real workflow hits `_ack_approve`. This asymmetry is acceptable for Phase 9.

## Phase 3: Event Normalization

### Step 5: Create normalization helper (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, *, run_dir: str) -> dict`:
   - Strips `"ts"`, `"hash"`.
   - Rewrites absolute paths: replaces literal run-dir prefix with `<RUN_DIR>/`.
   - Keeps all other structural fields (`kind`, `plan_step_id`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `plan_step_path`, `child_plan_hash`, `produces_check`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `on_exhaust`, etc.).
   - Sorts keys.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_file(path: Path, *, run_dir: str) -> list[dict]`:
   - Reads JSONL, applies `normalize_event` to each line, returns list of normalized dicts.

3. **Applied only to the golden** (persisted and in-memory diff copy). Live `events.jsonl` is untouched.

### Step 6: Import normalization directly — no `__init__.py` export
**Scope:** No-op — note: import as `from artagents.core.task.normalize import normalize_events_file` in cli.py.

## Phase 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` with Runtime Replay

### Step 7: Rewrite `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Large — replaces the Phase 5 scaffold body entirely.

1. **New function signature**:
```python
def _cmd_test(
    *,
    pack_id: str,
    orch_id: str,
    fixture_name: str,
    packs_root: Path,
    regenerate: bool = False,
) -> int:
```

2. **Runtime replay flow**:

   a. **Compile** (always): `compile_to_path(f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", packs_root=packs_root)`.

   b. **Locate**:
      - `pack_root = packs_root / pack_id`
      - `build_path = pack_root / "build" / f"{orch_id}.json"`
      - `orch_root = pack_root / orch_id`
      - `fixture_dir = orch_root / "fixtures" / fixture_name`
      - `golden_path = orch_root / "golden" / f"{fixture_name}.events.jsonl"`

   c. **Validate**: if `not fixture_dir.is_dir()`, print error and exit 2.

   d. **Scratch dir**: `scratch = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()`; `projects_root = Path(scratch.name)`. Slug is `"author-test-smoke"` (or `f"author-test-{fixture_name.replace('/', '-')}"`).

   e. **Fixture copy**: `import shutil; shutil.copytree(fixture_dir, projects_root / slug, dirs_exist_ok=True)`. This ensures fixture inputs (e.g., stub audio files for non-smoke fixtures) are present in the scratch project directory before `cmd_start`.

   f. **Start**: `cmd_start([f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", "--project", slug], packs_root=packs_root, projects_root=projects_root)`.

   g. **Read run_id**: `active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)`; `run_id = active_run["run_id"]`.

   h. **Environ snapshot/restore**: Save `os.environ` copies of TASK_RUN_ID, TASK_PROJECT, TASK_STEP_ID, TASK_ITEM_ID, TASK_ITERATION, ARTAGENTS_ACTOR, and AUTHOR_TEST_ENV. Set `os.environ[AUTHOR_TEST_ENV] = "1"`. Restore in `finally`.

   i. **Loop**:
      ```python
      while True:
          proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
          plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
          events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
          plan = load_plan(plan_path)
          events = read_events(events_path)
          peek = peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id)
          if peek.exhausted:
              break
          current_step = peek.step
          current_path = peek.path_tuple
          if isinstance(current_step, CodeStep):
              step_dir = step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, current_path, root=projects_root)
              step_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
              argv = shlex.split(current_step.command)
              subprocess.run(argv, cwd=step_dir, env=child_subprocess_env(), check=False)
              # gate_command for code steps executes step_dispatched + records completion
              decision = gate_command(slug, current_step.command, argv, root=projects_root)
              record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)
          elif isinstance(current_step, AttestedStep):
              token_kind = "agent" if current_step.ack.kind == "agent" else "actor"
              command_str = current_step.command + f" --{token_kind} author_test"
              argv = shlex.split(command_str)
              gate_command(slug, command_str, argv, root=projects_root)
          else:
              # NestedStep should not surface from peek_current_step
              break
      ```

      **Key details:**
      - `step_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` BEFORE `subprocess.run(cwd=step_dir)` — the gate does NOT create leaf step dirs (only `iter_dir` at gate.py:999).
      - The `command_str` for attested steps includes `--agent author_test` (or `--actor`) appended so `match_attested_command` strips it and the remainder matches `step.command`.
      - `child_subprocess_env()` scrubs `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` from code-step children.
      - For `CodeStep`, we call `gate_command` + `record_dispatch_complete` to emit the proper step_dispatched/step_completed events.
      - `peek_current_step` shares `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` with `gate_command`, so auto-traversal (nested plans, for_each, iteration) is handled consistently.

   j. **Normalize**: After loop, `normalize_events_file(events_path, run_dir=str(projects_root / slug))`.

   k. **Regenerate or diff**:
      - If `regenerate`: write normalized events as JSONL to `golden_path`, print confirmation, exit 0.
      - Else: load committed golden, normalize it, `difflib.unified_diff`. If match: exit 0. If drift: print diff, exit 1.

3. **Delete old scaffold body** (lines 311-371 in current `cli.py`).

### Step 8: Wire CLI flags into parser and `main` (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Update `_build_parser`** (replace test subparser):
```python
test_p = sub.add_parser("test", help="author test [<pack>.<name>] --fixture <name> [--regenerate]")
test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", nargs="?", default=None,
                    help="qualified id of the form <pack>.<name> (omit when using --pack/--orchestrator)")
test_p.add_argument("--pack", default=None, help="pack id")
test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", default=None, help="orchestrator name")
test_p.add_argument("--fixture", default=None, help="fixture name (under <pack>/<orch>/fixtures/)")
test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", default=False,
                    help="write current events as the new golden")
```

2. **Update `main`** (replace test dispatch):
```python
if args.cmd == "test":
    if args.pack and args.orchestrator:
        pack_id, orch_id = args.pack, args.orchestrator
    elif args.qualified_id:
        pack_id, orch_id = _qualified_split(args.qualified_id)
    else:
        _print_err("author test: --pack and --orchestrator are required when qualified id is not provided")
        return 2
    if not args.fixture:
        _print_err("author test: --fixture is required")
        return 2
    return _cmd_test(
        pack_id=pack_id,
        orch_id=orch_id,
        fixture_name=args.fixture,
        packs_root=_packs_root_arg(packs_root),
        regenerate=args.regenerate,
    )
```

3. **Update `pipeline.py` entrypoint help** (line 199): Change `--fixture <name>` to `--fixture <name> [--regenerate]` on the `author test` line.

### Step 9: Create `docs/AUTHORING.md`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** `docs/AUTHORING.md` from scratch (the file does not exist — see issue_hints-3, all_locations-1). Document:
   - `artagents author test` workflow: compile → run against fixture → diff against golden.
   - `--regenerate` flag: overwrites golden with current normalized events. Remind authors to commit.
   - Auto-approval mode (`ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`): attested steps auto-advance with synthetic `author_test` attestor.
   - `repeat.until(condition="user_approves")` behavior under auto-approval: auto-approves on iteration 1, loop terminates immediately. Goldens contain only iteration=1.
   - Fixture/golden path convention: `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
   - Namespacing convention: fixture names are orch-scoped by directory layout (no need for `hype/smoke` encoding).

**Scope:** Medium

Tests:

   b. **Without the env var** (via `monkeypatch.delenv`): attested step reaches `validate_attested_identity` and rejects missing `--agent`/`--actor`.
   c. **`child_subprocess_env()` scrubs `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`**: call `child_subprocess_env()` and assert the key is absent.
   d. **`canonical_event_json` unchanged**: verify it still strips only `hash`.
   e. **`ack.kind=actor`**: monkeypatches `step.ack.kind` to `"actor"`, verifies `attestor_kind="actor"` in the emitted event and the test-loop command string uses `--actor author_test` (callers-3 resolved).

## Phase 7: Validate

### Step 16: Run full test suite
**Scope:** Small

1. `pytest tests/ -x` (all existing + new tests pass).
2. `pytest tests/test_author_test_*.py -v` (new tests pass individually).
3. CLI smoke: `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0.

## Execution Order
1. Step 2 (fixture/golden dirs — create before anything else).
2. Step 1 (hype.py DSL orchestrator + compile).
3. Step 3 (env constant + scrubbing).
4. Step 4 (auto-approval, depends on Step 3 constant).
5. Step 5 (normalization).
6. Steps 7 + 8 (cli rewrite + flags).
7. Step 9 (docs).
8. Step 10 (generate + commit golden).
9. Step 11 (delete scaffold tests if present).
10. Steps 12–15 (new tests).
11. Step 16 (full validation).

## Key Design Decisions (Revision 4)

1. **Fixture/golden paths are orch-scoped**: `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl` — matches the brief's literal layout. The STOP CONDITION `--fixture smoke` works verbatim.
2. **Auto-approval wires through `gate_command`**, not the CLI `ack` path. The test loop constructs `command_str = step.command + ' --agent author_test'` so `match_attested_command` succeeds before the auto-approval branch fires.
3. **`step_dir` is mkdir'd** by the test loop (not the gate) before `subprocess.run(cwd=step_dir)`.
4. **`cmd_start` receives explicit kwargs**: `packs_root`, `projects_root`. Run_id is read from `active_run.json`.
5. **Fixture copy**: `shutil.copytree` before `cmd_start` ensures fixture inputs are present.
6. **Executor runner scrub**: only line 311 (the dict-merge + subprocess.run site) needs fixing; lines 304 and 319 are result-object assignments.
7. **No `source` field on events**: auto-approval marker lives in `evidence`.
8. **`docs/AUTHORING.md` is CREATED** from scratch.
9. **`test_author_test_pass.py` loads committed golden** — never regenerates.
10. **Known limitations**: lifecycle_ack asymmetry (scope-1), shlex on non-trivial attested commands (correctness-4), legacy `hype/run.py` env leak (correctness-3) — all documented, none blocking.

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 5)

## Overview

Phase 9 replaces the Phase 5 scaffold `_cmd_test` (which diffs pre-existing fixture vs golden events files) with a full runtime replay: compile the orchestrator, run the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON, capture live `events.jsonl`, normalize it, and diff against the committed golden. The `--regenerate` flag writes the current (normalized) events as the new golden.

**Fixture/golden path layout** matches the brief's literal spec: fixtures live at `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and goldens at `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`. The STOP CONDITION command `--pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` executes verbatim.

**Auto-approval** lives in `_dispatch_attested` (gate.py). When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, identity validation is skipped, attestor_kind comes from `step.ack.kind`, and attestor_id is `"author_test"`. The auto-approval marker goes into `evidence` as `"source:author_test"` — no changes to `canonical_event_json`.

**The test loop** calls `gate_command` directly for attested steps (not the CLI `ack` path), constructing the command string with `--agent author_test` (or `--actor` per `step.ack.kind`) so `match_attested_command` sees and strips the token before the auto-approval branch fires. Every `subprocess.run` passes `env=child_subprocess_env()` and the executor runner also scrubs `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`.

**Revision 5 fixes** address all 13 open significant flags from the gate critique:
- **Correctness-1 (reorder)**: code-step ordering fixed: gate_command first (sets TASK_* env), then subprocess.run, then record_dispatch_complete.
- **Correctness-2 (infinite-loop guard)**: max-iterations guard added — after 5 consecutive same-cursor visits the loop aborts.
- **Correctness-3 (missing golden)**: explicit friendly error before diff.
- **Correctness-4 (fixture collision)**: fixtures now copied into `inputs/` subdirectory.
- **Issue_hints-1 (build-path contradiction)**: Step 12 (tests) updated to `builtin/build/hype.json`.
- **_cmd_new layout cluster (6 flags)**: `_cmd_new` updated to scaffold the orch-scoped layout, not deferred.
- **Issue_hints-3 (stub audio)**: `inputs/input.txt` stub committed to exercise fixture-copy.
- Remaining defensive flags (correctness-4 residual) documented as known limitations.

## Known Limitations

1. **lifecycle_ack asymmetry (scope-1)**: `_ack_approve` does not honor `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`. The test loop calls `gate_command` directly, never the CLI `ack` path. Under author-test mode, no real workflow hits `_ack_approve`. Acceptable for Phase 9.
2. **shlex on non-trivial attested commands (correctness-4)**: The test loop constructs `command_str = step.command + ' --agent author_test'` and `shlex.split`s it. For commands with complex quoting (nested quotes, escaped characters), the split + match_attested_command may fail. The smoke fixture's command is simple; this已知 limitation is documented for future fixture authors.
3. **Legacy `hype/run.py` subprocess leak**: Known subprocess sites in legacy `hype/run.py` use bare `os.environ` and are out of scope for Phase 9. The new DSL orchestrator's test loop does not exercise legacy paths.
4. **Fixture directory constraint**: Fixtures must NOT contain files named `active_run.json`, `plan.json`, or a `runs/` subdirectory. Fixture contents are copied into `<project>/inputs/` to avoid collision.
5. **repeat.until user_approves**: Under auto-approval, attested steps auto-approve on iteration 1, satisfying the until condition, so the loop terminates immediately. Goldens contain only iteration=1. Authors must run non-auto-approval to test multi-iteration paths.

## Phase 1: Foundation — DSL Orchestrator, Fixture/Golden Dirs, Stub Inputs

### Step 1: Create fixture, golden, and stub input directories (orch-scoped)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** directories per the brief's literal layout:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/inputs/` — fixture inputs.
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/` — golden events.
2. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/inputs/input.txt` with content:
   ```
   stub input for smoke fixture — exercises fixture-copy path
   ```
   This is a small text stub per the brief's "a small audio file or stub" preference. It exercises the `shutil.copytree` path without adding binary content.

### Step 2: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` DSL orchestrator
**Scope:** Small

1. **Create** `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` with `plan_id = "builtin.hype"`. Two steps: one `code` that writes a JSON file, one `attested` with `ack.kind="agent"`:

```python
from artagents.orchestrate import attested, code, file_nonempty, plan

plan("builtin.hype", [
    code("gen", argv=["python3", "-c",
        "import json; json.dump({'ok': True}, open('out.json','w'))"
    ], produces={"out.json": file_nonempty()}),
    attested("review", instructions="reviewed by author_test",
             command="artagents ack review --decision approve",
             ack="agent"),
])
```

**Design:**
- `code` step writes `out.json` as a relative path. The test loop sets `cwd=step_dir` so the file lands at `step_dir/out.json`, which `_run_inline_checks` expects.
- `attested` step uses `ack="agent"`. Under auto-approval, the test loop constructs `command_str = "artagents ack review --decision approve --agent author_test"` and calls `gate_command`. `match_attested_command` strips `--agent author_test`, compares remainder to `step.command`, matches.
- `plan_id = "builtin.hype"` — exactly what the STOP CONDITION requires.
- `hype.py` coexists with `hype/` package. `resolve_orchestrator` loads via `spec_from_file_location` with a unique module name, fully isolated from the `builtin.hype` package.

2. **Compile**: `artagents author compile builtin.hype` → writes to `artagents/packs/builtin/build/hype.json` (per compile.py:142: `root / pack / "build" / f"{name}.json"`).

### Step 3: Update `_cmd_new` to scaffold orch-scoped fixture/golden layout
**Scope:** Small

1. **Update `_cmd_new`** in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py:265-283`:

   Replace the old pack-scoped layout:
   ```python
   fixtures_dir = pack_root / "fixtures" / name
   golden_dir = pack_root / "golden"
   fixtures_keep = fixtures_dir / ".keep"
   golden_events = golden_dir / f"{name}.events.jsonl"
   ```

   With the new orch-scoped layout matching `_cmd_test`:
   ```python
   orch_fixtures_dir = pack_root / name / "fixtures" / "smoke" / "inputs"
   orch_golden_dir = pack_root / name / "golden"
   fixtures_keep = orch_fixtures_dir / ".keep"
   golden_events = orch_golden_dir / "smoke.events.jsonl"
   ```

   And update the `mkdir` lines accordingly:
   ```python
   orch_fixtures_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   orch_golden_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   ```

   Update printed paths to use actual `created` paths (already handled by `relative_to` logic).

2. **Update `test_author_cli.py:192-193`** to assert the new orch-scoped layout:
   - Change `pack_root / "fixtures" / name / ".keep"` to `pack_root / name / "fixtures" / "smoke" / "inputs" / ".keep"`
   - Add/update any related golden path assertions.

## Phase 2: Core — Auto-Approval Mode + Env Scrubbing

### Step 4: Add `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` constant and scrub from child env (`artagents/core/task/env.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** constant at module level (after line 13):
```python
AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"
```

2. **Modify `child_subprocess_env`** (line 68): Add to the pop list:
```python
env.pop(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV, None)
```

### Step 5: Fix executor runner env leak (`artagents/core/executor/runner.py:311`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Modify line 311**: After the dict merge, pop the env var:
```python
full_env = {**os.environ, **env, **_project_subprocess_env(request), **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()}
full_env.pop("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST", None)
```
Then use `full_env` in `subprocess.run(... env=full_env ...)`.

### Step 6: Add auto-approval in `_dispatch_attested` (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add** import at top of `gate.py`:
```python
from artagents.core.task.env import AUTHOR_TEST_ENV

def _is_author_test_mode() -> bool:
    return os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV, "") == "1"
```
(Add `import os` if not already imported.)

2. **Modify `_dispatch_attested`** (lines 918-927):

Replace lines 921-927 (the `validate_attested_identity` call and `evidence` assignment):
```python
    matched, args = match_attested_command(command, step.command)
    if not matched:
        _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)

    if _is_author_test_mode():
        # Auto-approval: skip identity validation, synthesize attestation.
        attestor_kind = step.ack.kind
        attestor_id = "author_test"
        evidence = ("source:author_test",) + args.evidence
    else:
        attestor_kind, attestor_id = validate_attested_identity(
            slug=slug,
            step=step,
            args=args,
            run_started_actor=run_started_actor,
        )
        evidence = args.evidence
```

The existing event-emitting code (lines 929-944) already uses `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, and `args.evidence`. Update to use `evidence` (the local) instead of `args.evidence`:

```python
    if item_id is not None:
        append_event(
            events_path,
            make_item_attested_event(
                path_tuple,
                item_id,
                attestor_kind=attestor_kind,
                attestor_id=attestor_id,
                evidence=evidence,
            ),
        )
    else:
        append_event(
            events_path,
            make_step_attested_event(path_str, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence),
        )
```

**Design decision:** The auto-approval marker `"source:author_test"` lives in `evidence`, which is already part of the event payload and appears in normalized diffs. No changes to `canonical_event_json`. No `source` field on events — no schema collision risk.

## Phase 3: Event Normalization

### Step 7: Create normalization helper (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, *, run_dir: str) -> dict`:
   - Strips `"ts"`, `"hash"`.
   - Rewrites absolute paths: replaces literal run-dir prefix with `<RUN_DIR>/`.
   - Keeps all other structural fields (`kind`, `plan_step_id`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `plan_step_path`, `child_plan_hash`, `produces_check`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `on_exhaust`, etc.).
   - Sorts keys.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_file(path: Path, *, run_dir: str) -> list[dict]`:
   - Reads JSONL, applies `normalize_event` to each line, returns list of normalized dicts.

3. **Applied only to the golden** (persisted and in-memory diff copy). Live `events.jsonl` is untouched.

**Scope:** No-op — note: import as `from artagents.core.task.normalize import normalize_events_file` in cli.py.

## Phase 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` with Runtime Replay

### Step 9: Rewrite `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Large — replaces the Phase 5 scaffold body entirely (lines 311-371).

1. **New function signature**:
```python
def _cmd_test(
    *,
    pack_id: str,
    orch_id: str,
    fixture_name: str,
    packs_root: Path,
    regenerate: bool = False,
) -> int:
```

2. **Runtime replay flow**:

   a. **Compile** (always): `compile_to_path(f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", packs_root=packs_root)`.

   b. **Locate**:
      - `pack_root = packs_root / pack_id`
      - `build_path = pack_root / "build" / f"{orch_id}.json"` (matches compile.py:142)
      - `orch_root = pack_root / orch_id`
      - `fixture_dir = orch_root / "fixtures" / fixture_name`
      - `golden_path = orch_root / "golden" / f"{fixture_name}.events.jsonl"`

   c. **Validate**: if `not fixture_dir.is_dir()`, print error and exit 2.

   d. **Scratch dir**: `scratch = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()`; `projects_root = Path(scratch.name)`. Slug is `f"author-test-{fixture_name}"`.

   e. **Fixture copy into `inputs/` subdirectory**:
      ```python
      import shutil
      project_slug_dir = projects_root / slug
      inputs_dir = project_slug_dir / "inputs"
      shutil.copytree(fixture_dir, inputs_dir, dirs_exist_ok=True)
      ```
      This avoids collision with `active_run.json`, `plan.json`, and `runs/` that `cmd_start` writes into the project slug directory itself.

   f. **Start**: `cmd_start([f"{pack_id}.{orch_id}", "--project", slug], packs_root=packs_root, projects_root=projects_root)`.

   g. **Read run_id**: `active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)`; `run_id = active_run["run_id"]`.

   h. **Environ snapshot/restore**: Save `os.environ` copies of TASK_RUN_ID, TASK_PROJECT, TASK_STEP_ID, TASK_ITEM_ID, TASK_ITERATION, ARTAGENTS_ACTOR, and AUTHOR_TEST_ENV. Set `os.environ[AUTHOR_TEST_ENV] = "1"`. Restore in `finally`.

   i. **Loop** with max-iterations guard:
      ```python
      MAX_LOOP_ITERATIONS = 100
      MAX_SAME_CURSOR_STREAK = 5
      same_cursor_count = 0
      last_cursor: tuple[str, ...] | None = None

      for loop_iter in range(MAX_LOOP_ITERATIONS):
          proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
          plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
          events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
          plan = load_plan(plan_path)
          events = read_events(events_path)
          peek = peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id)
          if peek.exhausted:
              break
          current_step = peek.step
          current_path = peek.path_tuple

          # Max-same-cursor guard: abort if produces_check_failed causes infinite rewind.
          if current_path == last_cursor:
              same_cursor_count += 1
              if same_cursor_count > MAX_SAME_CURSOR_STREAK:
                  _print_err(f"author test: produces check failing repeatedly at cursor {current_path}; aborting")
                  return 1
          else:
              same_cursor_count = 0
          last_cursor = current_path

          if isinstance(current_step, CodeStep):
              # Correct ordering: gate_command FIRST (emits step_dispatched, applies TASK_* env),
              # then subprocess.run (child_subprocess_env captures TASK_*), then record_dispatch_complete.
              decision = gate_command(slug, current_step.command, shlex.split(current_step.command), root=projects_root)
              step_dir = step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, current_path, root=projects_root)
              step_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
              result = subprocess.run(current_step.command.split(), cwd=step_dir, env=child_subprocess_env(), check=False)
              record_dispatch_complete(decision, result.returncode)
          elif isinstance(current_step, AttestedStep):
              token_kind = "agent" if current_step.ack.kind == "agent" else "actor"
              command_str = current_step.command + f" --{token_kind} author_test"
              argv = shlex.split(command_str)
              gate_command(slug, command_str, argv, root=projects_root)
          else:
              # NestedStep should not surface from peek_current_step
              break
      else:
          _print_err(f"author test: test loop exceeded {MAX_LOOP_ITERATIONS} iterations; possible infinite loop")
          return 1
      ```

      **Key details:**
      - `gate_command` called FIRST for code steps → `_dispatch_code` calls `apply_task_run_env` which sets TASK_RUN_ID/TASK_PROJECT/TASK_STEP_ID on `os.environ`. Then `subprocess.run(cwd=step_dir, env=child_subprocess_env())` captures those env vars.
      - `step_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` BEFORE `subprocess.run(cwd=step_dir)`.
      - The `command_str` for attested steps includes `--agent author_test` (or `--actor`) appended so `match_attested_command` strips it and the remainder matches `step.command`.
      - `child_subprocess_env()` scrubs `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` and `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` from code-step children.
      - `record_dispatch_complete` emits `step_completed` and runs inline produces checks.
      - `peek_current_step` shares `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` with `gate_command`, so auto-traversal (nested plans, for_each, iteration) is handled consistently.
      - `MAX_SAME_CURSOR_STREAK = 5` guard catches `produces_check_failed` → cursor_rewind cycles that would otherwise loop forever.

   j. **Normalize**: After loop, `normalize_events_file(events_path, run_dir=str(projects_root / slug))`.

   k. **Regenerate or diff**:
      - If `regenerate`: write normalized events as JSONL to `golden_path` (parent dirs created), print `"golden regenerated at <golden_path> — commit this file if the change is intentional"`, exit 0.
      - Else:
        - If `not golden_path.exists()`: print `"no golden file at <golden_path>; run with --regenerate to create it"` (stderr), exit 2.
        - Load committed golden, normalize it with same `run_dir` → in-memory normalized golden.
        - `difflib.unified_diff(norm_golden_lines, norm_live_lines, ...)`.
        - If match: `print("ok ...")`, exit 0.
        - If drift: print diff, exit 1.

3. **Delete old scaffold body** (lines 311-371 in current `cli.py`) and the `_strip_volatile` helper (lines 287-308) — replaced by `normalize_events_file`.

```python
if args.cmd == "test":
    if args.pack and args.orchestrator:
        pack_id, orch_id = args.pack, args.orchestrator
    elif args.qualified_id:
        pack_id, orch_id = _qualified_split(args.qualified_id)
    else:
        _print_err("author test: --pack and --orchestrator are required when qualified id is not provided")
        return 2
    if not args.fixture:
        _print_err("author test: --fixture is required")
        return 2
    return _cmd_test(
        pack_id=pack_id,
        orch_id=orch_id,
        fixture_name=args.fixture,
        packs_root=_packs_root_arg(packs_root),
        regenerate=args.regenerate,
    )
```

### Step 11: Create `docs/AUTHORING.md`

1. **Create** `docs/AUTHORING.md` from scratch. Document:
   - `artagents author test` workflow: compile → run against fixture → diff against golden.
   - `--regenerate` flag: overwrites golden with current normalized events. Remind authors to commit.
   - Auto-approval mode (`ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`): attested steps auto-advance with synthetic `"author_test"` attestor.
   - `repeat.until(condition="user_approves")` behavior under auto-approval: auto-approves on iteration 1, loop terminates immediately. Goldens contain only iteration=1.
   - Fixture/golden path convention: `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
   - Namespacing convention: fixture names are orch-scoped by directory layout (no need for `hype/smoke` encoding).
   - Fixture constraints: must NOT contain `active_run.json`, `plan.json`, or `runs/` (fixture contents are copied to `<project>/inputs/`).

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 (fixture/golden dirs + stub input — create before anything else).
2. Step 2 (hype.py DSL orchestrator + compile).
3. Step 3 (_cmd_new layout update + test_author_cli.py update).
4. Step 4 (env constant + child_subprocess_env scrub).

## Key Design Decisions (Revision 5)

1. **Fixture/golden paths are orch-scoped**: `<pack>/<orch>/fixtures/<fixture>/` and `<pack>/<orch>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`. `_cmd_new` updated to match.
2. **Code-step ordering fixed**: `gate_command` (applies TASK_* env) → `subprocess.run` (captures env) → `record_dispatch_complete`.
3. **Max-iterations guard**: `MAX_SAME_CURSOR_STREAK = 5` prevents infinite loop on `produces_check_failed` → cursor_rewind cycles.
4. **Missing golden**: explicit friendly error + exit 2 (no bare FileNotFoundError).
5. **Fixture collision**: copied into `<project>/inputs/` subdirectory; reserved filenames documented.
6. **Stub input**: `inputs/input.txt` committed to exercise `shutil.copytree`.
7. **Auto-approval wires through `gate_command`**, not the CLI `ack` path. `command_str = step.command + ' --agent author_test'` so `match_attested_command` succeeds.
8. **`step_dir` is mkdir'd** by the test loop before `subprocess.run(cwd=step_dir)`.
9. **`cmd_start` receives explicit kwargs**: `packs_root`, `projects_root`. Run_id read from `active_run.json`.
10. **Executor runner scrub**: line 311 fix (only production subprocess site needing it).
11. **No `source` field on events**: auto-approval marker lives in `evidence`.
12. **`docs/AUTHORING.md` is CREATED** from scratch.
13. **`test_author_test_pass.py` loads committed golden** — never regenerates.
14. **All 13 open flags addressed** — no deferred layout inconsistency.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

This is critique iteration 5. The template file includes prior findings with their status. Verify addressed flags were actually fixed, re-flag if inadequate, and check for new issues introduced by the revision.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.

        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.

        - Focus on concrete issues, not structural formatting.

> AGENT

Critique iteration 5 submitted via StructuredOutput.

**Verified resolved (17 flags):** All 13 prior open significant flags are now properly addressed. Code-step ordering fixed (gate_command first, then subprocess), max-iteration guard added, missing-golden UX has friendly error path, fixture copy isolated to `inputs/` subdirectory, _cmd_new now scaffolds the orch-scoped layout, test_author_cli.py assertions updated, build path corrected to `builtin/build/hype.json`, stub input committed.

**New issues / open:**

- **FLAG-P9-029 (significant)**: Plan Step 9i uses `current_step.command.split()` (naive whitespace split) for subprocess.run while using `shlex.split(current_step.command)` for gate_command on the same line. Since `CodeStep.command` is `shlex.join`'d at compile time (dsl.py:404), the smoke fixture's quoted argv `python3 -c "..."` will be mis-split and the subprocess will fail. Must use `shlex.split` for both.
- **FLAG-P9-030**: `_cmd_new` now hardcodes fixture name `'smoke'`. Reasonable convention, but should be called out explicitly in AUTHORING.md.
- **FLAG-P9-031**: Plan doesn't address existing packs that may have been scaffolded under the legacy pack-scoped layout. Those become orphaned post-migration. Audit needed.
- **FLAG-P9-032/033**: `MAX_SAME_CURSOR_STREAK = 5` and `MAX_LOOP_ITERATIONS = 100` could false-positive on legitimate complex orchestrators. Document in AUTHORING.md or size more generously.
- **FLAG-P9-034**: Step 1 commits `inputs/input.txt` while Step 14 test setup says `inputs/.keep` — documentation inconsistency without functional impact.

The plan is now structurally sound — only one significant correctness bug remains (FLAG-P9-029, the `.split()` vs `shlex.split()` bug); the rest are minor polish/documentation concerns.
